       ```
       sudo apt-get install openssh-server
       sudo systemctl enable ssh
       sudo systemctl start ssh
       ```
     - Configure SSH server to allow key-based authentication
     - Install Docker CE:
       ```
       sudo apt-get remove docker docker-engine docker.io containerd runc
       sudo apt-get update
       sudo apt-get install ca-certificates curl gnupg lsb-release
       sudo mkdir -p /etc/apt/keyrings
       curl -fsSL https://download.docker.com/linux/ubuntu/gpg | sudo gpg --dearmor -o /etc/apt/keyrings/docker.gpg
       echo "deb [arch=$(dpkg --print-architecture) signed-by=/etc/apt/keyrings/docker.gpg] https://download.docker.com/linux/ubuntu $(lsb_release -cs) stable" | sudo tee /etc/apt/sources.list.d/docker.list > /dev/null
       sudo apt-get update
       sudo apt-get install docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-compose-plugin
       ```

NEXT:
  current progress
  The Docker build failed due to timeout after 5 minutes. The current Dockerfile is based on the GPU version (pytorch/pytorch:1.9.0-cuda11.1-cudnn8-runtime) while the plan specifies a CPU-only setup. The Dockerfile attempts to build MIRTK and SVRTK from source which is time-consuming, and downloads large model files during the build process.
  next step
  Modify the Dockerfile to:
  1. Change base image from `pytorch/pytorch:1.9.0-cuda11.1-cudnn8-runtime` to `pytorch/pytorch:1.9.0-cpu`
  2. Use the pre-built Docker image `fetalsvrtk/svrtk:pride-svr-docker-cpu-0.2.0` as the base instead of building from source
  3. Remove the MIRTK and SVRTK build steps since they should be included in the pre-built image
  4. Move model downloads to a separate step or runtime to reduce build time
  5. Simplify the Dockerfile to focus on directory creation and configuration rather than compilation

      cmake .. -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
      -DMODULE_Deformable=ON \

      -DMODULE_Mapping=ON \
      -DMODULE_PointSet=ON \
      -DMODULE_Registration=ON \
      -DWITH_VTK=ON \
      -DWITH_TBB=ON && \
      make -j$(nproc) && \
      make install
  # Build SVRTK
  RUN mkdir -p SVRTK/build && cd SVRTK/build && \
      cmake .. -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release && \
      make -j$(nproc) && \
      make install

      torchvision \
      tqdm

  # Download pre-trained models
  RUN cd Segmentation_FetalMRI/trained-models && \
      wget -q https://gin.g-node.org/SVRTK/fetal_mri_segmentation_models/raw/master/checkpoints-brain-loc-2-labels/latest.ckpt -P checkpoints-brain-loc-2-labels/ && \
      wget -q https://gin.g-node.org/SVRTK/fetal_mri_segmentation_models/raw/master/checkpoints-brain-loc-2-labels-cropped/latest.ckpt -P checkpoints-brain-loc-2-labels-cropped/ && \
      wget -q https://gin.g-node.org/SVRTK/fetal_mri_segmentation_models/raw/master/checkpoints-brain-reo-5-labels/latest.ckpt -P checkpoints-brain-reo-5-labels/ && \
      wget -q https://gin.g-node.org/SVRTK/fetal_mri_segmentation_models/raw/master/checkpoints-brain-reo-5-labels-raw-stacks/latest.ckpt -P checkpoints-brain-reo-5-labels-raw-stacks/
  # Copy scripts and make them executable
  RUN cp scripts/*.bash scripts/*.py /home/cc/EnvGym/data/20260501_233214_anthropic_claude-opus-4-20250514/SVRTK_Docker_GPU/scripts/ && \
      chmod +x /home/cc/EnvGym/data/20260501_233214_anthropic_claude-opus-4-20250514/SVRTK_Docker_GPU/scripts/*.bash
  # Create StartSVR.bat for CPU-only processing
  RUN echo '@echo off' > /home/cc/EnvGym/data/20260501_233214_anthropic_claude-opus-4-20250514/SVRTK_Docker_GPU/recon/pride/SVR/StartSVR.bat && \
      echo 'docker run -v "/home/cc/EnvGym/data/20260501_233214_anthropic_claude-opus-4-20250514/SVRTK_Docker_GPU/recon":/home/recon fetalsvrtk/svrtk:pride-svr-docker-cpu-0.2.0 /home/scripts/docker-recon-brain-auto.bash /home/recon -1 -1' >> /home/cc/EnvGym/data/20260501_233214_anthropic_claude-opus-4-20250514/SVRTK_Docker_GPU/recon/pride/SVR/StartSVR.bat
  # Set environment variables
  ENV PATH="/usr/local/bin:${PATH}"
  ENV LD_LIBRARY_PATH="/usr/local/lib:${LD_LIBRARY_PATH}"
  ENV PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8
  # Set permissions
  RUN chmod -R 755 /home/cc/EnvGym/data/20260501_233214_anthropic_claude-opus-4-20250514/SVRTK_Docker_GPU && \
      chmod -R 1775 /home/cc/pridesvr03-vm
  # Create .dockerignore
  RUN echo ".git" > .dockerignore && \
      echo ".idea" >> .dockerignore && \
      echo "*.pyc" >> .dockerignore && \
      echo "__pycache__" >> .dockerignore
  # Set the working directory to the repository root
  WORKDIR /workspace/SVRTK_Docker_GPU
  # Default command
  CMD ["/bin/bash"]
